    Eq.{u_3 + 1} (ContinuousLinearMap.comp.{0, 0, 0, u_3, u_4, u_3} (ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint.{0, u_3, u_4} K) K)
        (ContinuousLinearMap.id.{0, u_3} ℂ G) →

        (∀ (a b : Fin 2 → ↥(unitary.{u_1} (CGLMP5.Op.{u_1} H))),

              (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (b i) 5) 1) →
                (∀ (i j : Fin 2), Commute.{u_1} (a i) (b j)) →
                  LE.le.{u_1} (CGLMP5.SOS.bell.{u_1} ω a b) (HSMul.hSMul.{0, u_1, u_1} (↑μ) 1)) →
          ∀ (A B : Fin 2 → CGLMP5.PVM.{u_1} (CGLMP5.Op.{u_1} H)),
            (∀ (x y : Fin 2) (a b : Fin 5),

@CGLMP5.tensor_pvm_bound_of_unitary_bound.{u_1,
    u_2} : ∀ {H : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup.{u_1} H] [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace.{0, u_1} ℂ H]
  [inst_2 : CompleteSpace.{u_1} H] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup.{u_2} K] [inst_4 : InnerProductSpace.{0, u_2} ℂ K]
  [inst_5 : CompleteSpace.{u_2} K] (μ : ℝ) (ω : ℂ),
  Eq.{1} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (Star.star.{0} ω) ω) 1 →
    Eq.{1} (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} ω 5) 1 →
      Eq.{1}
          (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
            (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} 1 ω) (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} ω 2))
              (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} ω 3))
            (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} ω 4))
          0 →
        (∀ (a b : Fin 2 → ↥(unitary.{max u_2 u_1} (CGLMP5.Op.{max u_2 u_1} (CGLMP5.HTensor.{u_1, u_2} H K)))),
            (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{(max u_1 u_2) + 1} (HPow.hPow.{max u_1 u_2, 0, max u_1 u_2} (a i) 5) 1) →
              (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{(max u_1 u_2) + 1} (HPow.hPow.{max u_1 u_2, 0, max u_1 u_2} (b i) 5) 1) →
                (∀ (i j : Fin 2), Commute.{max u_1 u_2} (a i) (b j)) →
                  LE.le.{max u_1 u_2} (CGLMP5.SOS.bell.{max u_1 u_2} ω a b)
                    (HSMul.hSMul.{0, max u_1 u_2, max u_1 u_2} (↑μ) 1)) →
          ∀ (φ : CGLMP5.State.{max u_2 u_1} (CGLMP5.HTensor.{u_1, u_2} H K))

            (B : CGLMP5.Setting → CGLMP5.PVM.{u_2} (CGLMP5.Op.{u_2} K)),

              (CGLMP5.cglmp
                (CGLMP5.jointProbability.{u_1, u_2} φ (fun x => CGLMP5.PVM.toPOVM.{u_1} (A x)) fun y =>

CGLMP5.SOS.compact_identity
@CGLMP5.SOS.compact_identity.{u_1} : ∀ {R : Type u_1} [inst : Ring.{u_1} R] [inst_1 : StarRing.{u_1} R]
  [inst_2 : Algebra.{0, u_1} ℂ R] [StarModule.{0, u_1} ℂ R] (a b : Fin 2 → ↥(unitary.{u_1} R)),
  (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (a i) 5) 1) →
    (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (b i) 5) 1) →
      (∀ (i j : Fin 2), Commute.{u_1} (a i) (b j)) →
        Eq.{u_1 + 1}
          (HSub.hSub.{u_1, u_1, u_1} (HSMul.hSMul.{0, u_1, u_1} (↑CGLMP5.mu) 1)
            (CGLMP5.SOS.bell.{u_1} (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} CGLMP5.zeta 4) a b))
          (∑ j,
            HSMul.hSMul.{0, u_1, u_1} (↑(CGLMP5.weightValue j))
              (HAdd.hAdd.{u_1, u_1, u_1}
                (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} (Star.star.{u_1} (CGLMP5.SOS.realizingPolynomial.{u_1} a b j))
                  (CGLMP5.SOS.realizingPolynomial.{u_1} a b j))
                (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} (CGLMP5.SOS.realizingPolynomial.{u_1} a b j)
                  (Star.star.{u_1} (CGLMP5.SOS.realizingPolynomial.{u_1} a b j)))))
'CGLMP5.SOS.compact_identity' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice.{u}, Quot.sound.{u}]

CGLMP5.commuting_pvm_sos_identity
@CGLMP5.commuting_pvm_sos_identity.{u_1} : ∀ {H : Type u_1} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup.{u_1} H]
  [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace.{0, u_1} ℂ H] [inst_2 : CompleteSpace.{u_1} H]
  (A B : Fin 2 → CGLMP5.PVM.{u_1} (CGLMP5.Op.{u_1} H)),
  (∀ (x y : Fin 2) (a b : Fin 5), Commute.{u_1} (CGLMP5.PVM.effect.{u_1} (A x) a) (CGLMP5.PVM.effect.{u_1} (B y) b)) →

CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate_gramExpand

  [inst_2 : Algebra.{0, u_1} ℂ R] [StarModule.{0, u_1} ℂ R] (a b : Fin 2 → ↥(unitary.{u_1} R)),
  (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (a i) 5) 1) →
    (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (b i) 5) 1) →
      (∀ (i j : Fin 2), Commute.{u_1} (a i) (b j)) →
        ∀ (d : ℂ) (p : CGLMP5.SOS.Polynomial),
          Eq.{u_1 + 1} (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b (CGLMP5.SOS.gramExpand d p))
            (HSMul.hSMul.{0, u_1, u_1} d
              (HAdd.hAdd.{u_1, u_1, u_1}
                (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1} (Star.star.{u_1} (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b p))
                  (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b p))

CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate_factoredGramExpand
@CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate_factoredGramExpand.{u_1, u_2,
    u_3} : ∀ {R : Type u_1} [inst : Ring.{u_1} R] [inst_1 : StarRing.{u_1} R] [inst_2 : Algebra.{0, u_1} ℂ R]
  [StarModule.{0, u_1} ℂ R] (a b : Fin 2 → ↥(unitary.{u_1} R)),
  (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (a i) 5) 1) →
    (∀ (i : Fin 2), Eq.{u_1 + 1} (HPow.hPow.{u_1, 0, u_1} (b i) 5) 1) →
      (∀ (i j : Fin 2), Commute.{u_1} (a i) (b j)) →
        ∀ {Core : Type u_2} {Phase : Type u_3} (d : ℂ) (core : Core → ℂ) (phase : Phase → ℂ) (gram : Core → Core → ℂ)
          (phasePair : Phase → Phase → ℂ),
          (∀ (c e : Core),
              Eq.{1} (gram c e) (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} d (Star.star.{0} (core c))) (core e))) →
            (∀ (p q : Phase), Eq.{1} (phasePair p q) (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (Star.star.{0} (phase p)) (phase q))) →
              ∀ (entries : List.{max u_3 u_2} (Prod.{0, max u_3 u_2} CGLMP5.Word (Prod.{u_2, u_3} Core Phase))),
                Eq.{u_1 + 1}
                  (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b (CGLMP5.SOS.factoredGramExpand.{u_2, u_3} gram phasePair entries))
                  (HSMul.hSMul.{0, u_1, u_1} d
                    (HAdd.hAdd.{u_1, u_1, u_1}
                      (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1}
                        (Star.star.{u_1}
                          (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b (CGLMP5.SOS.phasePolynomial.{u_2, u_3} core phase entries)))
                        (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b (CGLMP5.SOS.phasePolynomial.{u_2, u_3} core phase entries)))
                      (HMul.hMul.{u_1, u_1, u_1}
                        (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b (CGLMP5.SOS.phasePolynomial.{u_2, u_3} core phase entries))
                        (Star.star.{u_1}
                          (CGLMP5.SOS.evaluate.{u_1} a b (CGLMP5.SOS.phasePolynomial.{u_2, u_3} core phase entries))))))

CGLMP5.tensorEuclideanEquiv
CGLMP5.tensorEuclideanEquiv.{u_1,
  u_2} : (m : Type u_1) →
  (n : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Fintype.{u_1} m] →
      [inst_1 : Fintype.{u_2} n] →
        [DecidableEq.{u_1 + 1} m] →
          [DecidableEq.{u_2 + 1} n] →
            LinearIsometryEquiv.{0, 0, max u_2 u_1, max u_2 u_1} (RingHom.id.{0} ℂ)
              (CGLMP5.HTensor.{u_1, u_2} (EuclideanSpace.{0, u_1} ℂ m) (EuclideanSpace.{0, u_2} ℂ n))
              (EuclideanSpace.{0, max u_2 u_1} ℂ (Prod.{u_1, u_2} m n))

CGLMP5.tensorEuclideanEquiv_operator_kronecker
@CGLMP5.tensorEuclideanEquiv_operator_kronecker.{u_1,
    u_2} : ∀ {m : Type u_1} {n : Type u_2} [inst : Fintype.{u_1} m] [inst_1 : Fintype.{u_2} n]
  [inst_2 : DecidableEq.{u_1 + 1} m] [inst_3 : DecidableEq.{u_2 + 1} n] (A : Matrix.{u_1, u_1, 0} m m ℂ)
  (B : Matrix.{u_2, u_2, 0} n n ℂ),
  Eq.{max (u_1 + 1) (u_2 + 1)}
    ((LinearIsometryEquiv.conjStarAlgEquiv.{0, max u_1 u_2, max u_1 u_2} (CGLMP5.tensorEuclideanEquiv.{u_1, u_2} m n))
      (CGLMP5.tensorMap.{u_1, u_1, u_2, u_2} (CGLMP5.matrixOp.{u_1} A) (CGLMP5.matrixOp.{u_2} B)))

      (Matrix.kroneckerMap.{0, 0, 0, u_1, u_1, u_2, u_2} (fun x1 x2 => HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} x1 x2) A B))

  u_2} : ∀ (m : Type u_1) (n : Type u_2) [inst : Fintype.{u_1} m] [inst_1 : Fintype.{u_2} n]

  u_2} : ∀ (m : Type u_1) (n : Type u_2) [inst : Fintype.{u_1} m] [inst_1 : Fintype.{u_2} n]
